Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the technologies object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 884. The moduli space has dimension at most 182 with 1 connected components.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the technologies space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 884 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 182. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
